- **shanghai-diff-explain**: 差分を意味的に完結した最小の改善へ分解し、依存関係に沿って構成するスキルです。上海式の差分解説や、コミットの構成、変更のアトミックな分割と説明順序の整理を求められた際に使用します。 ([MirrgieRiana/MirrgieRiana.github.io](https://github.com/MirrgieRiana/MirrgieRiana.github.io/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/shanghai-diff-explain))
- **shanghai-g6-tutor**: 上海六年级（初中预备班）语数英三科私人家教。对齐上海校内教材进度（统编语文、沪教数学、牛津上海/新世纪英语），采用引导式教学——只引导不代做，带错题本、进度追踪、间隔复习和家长周报。当用户需要陪孩子写作业、讲解错题、预习复习、出练习题、考前冲刺、默写听写、查看学习进度或生成家长报告时触发。触发词：辅导作业、讲题、这道题、错题、预习、复习、出题、默写、听写、月考、期中期末、学习报告、家长报告、六年级、预初。 ([r9412460971-cloud/OPC-skill](https://github.com/r9412460971-cloud/OPC-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/shanghai-g6-tutor) · ★37)
- **shanghai-gaokao-english-tutor**: 上海普通高考英语一对一教学与分数目标诊断：按年份、真实作答和评分来源评估“离140分还有多少”，记录115＋25＋10分项与估分区间，进行分级提示、独立迁移复测，在Obsidian持久保存学情。用于“上海高考英语老师”“诊断薄弱点”“比较模考”“阅读全对够140吗”“练概要／翻译”“继续英语复习”；不替代CET、雅思教练，不凭微题预测高考总分。 ([sylviachenxy/sylvia-agent-skills](https://github.com/sylviachenxy/sylvia-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/sylvia-learning/skills/shanghai-gaokao-english-tutor))

- **shanghainese**: Transform text into Shanghainese （上海話 / 滬語） — the Wu Chinese variety spoken in Shanghai, belonging to the Taihu subgroup （太湖片） of Wu （吳語）. Features Wu pronouns （儂/阿拉/伊/伊拉）, Wu negation （弗/勿/覅）, sentence-final particles （呃/嘞/伐/哦呦）, Wu adverbs （蠻/老/嘎）, signature lexicon （結棍/霞氣/嗲/戇/洋盤/淘漿糊/軋鬧猛/噶三胡）, cultural vocabulary （弄堂/石庫門/阿婆/爺叔/小囡）, and 海派 register（派頭/有腔調）. Three intensity levels from light Wu-flavored Mandarin (sprinkled markers) up to heavy 滬語書寫 (in the manner of 金宇澄《繁花》). Useful for creative writing, cultural appreciation, language education, or dialogue craft. Triggers on "/shanghainese"… ([tzengyuxio/skills](https://github.com/tzengyuxio/skills/tree/HEAD/shanghainese) · ★46)

- **shanghan-lun**: 来自《伤寒论》（张仲景）的知识技能包。适用于按张仲景的框架分析问题、检索书中概念、或把书中方法落到具体决策时调用。 ([jiyideweidao/tcm-jingfang-skill](https://github.com/jiyideweidao/tcm-jingfang-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/shanghan-lun))
- **shanghan-lun-yuanjin**: 基于《伤寒论》（张仲景，宋本）公有领域原文蒸馏而成的原典推演Skill，完整内置六经辨证体系、传变规律、条文释义、方证对应、脉证模型，支持原文条文解读、仲景医理考据、伤寒与金匮源流对比。严格剥离后世各家注家发挥、现代中医临床诊疗经验，忠于宋本原文文本。仅用于古籍医理学习推演，严禁输出辨证开方、用药剂量、治疗方案、个人疾病诊疗建议。本skill应在用户要求解读伤寒原文条文、学习六经理论、考据方证本义、对比伤寒与金匮思想时使用；涉及个人看病、辨证处方、指导服药、疾病治疗场景时不得使用。This skill is for studying the original text of Shang Han Lun, and does not provide any disease diagnosis, prescription or medication guidance. - **SHAP**: SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations) is a unified, game-theoretic framework for local feature attribution. Treating a model's input features as players in a coalition game, SHAP assigns each feature its Shapley value — the average marginal contribution of that feature to the prediction across all possible feature subsets. Shapley values are the unique attributions satisfying local accuracy, missingness, and consistency, so the prediction decomposes additively into a baseline plus one Shapley value per feature, giving a per-prediction explanation that is theoretically grounded and model-agnost… ([zjunlp/Mechanist](https://github.com/zjunlp/Mechanist/tree/HEAD/skills/mechanism-skills/SHAP) · ★82 · 2 copies)

([ardent-ai/ardent-claude-plugins](https://github.com/ardent-ai/ardent-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/ardent/skills/shaping))
- **shaping**: Shape a feature before building it. Iterate on both the problem (requirements) and solution (shapes) before committing to implementation. Use when facing new features, ambiguous requests, or multiple valid approaches. Produces fit checks, breadboards, and vertical slices. ([belumontoya/tools](https://github.com/belumontoya/tools/tree/HEAD/claude-skills/.claude/skills/shaping))
- **shaping**: Shaping - turn a raw idea for a change into a pitch, after Shape Up: the problem, the solution, its no-gos and rabbit holes. ([camille-hdl/cmdbaby](https://github.com/camille-hdl/cmdbaby/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/shaping) · 2 copies)
- **shaping**: Use at the START of a half-baked idea — a new feature, service, refactor, migration, rewrite, re-platforming, technology switch, or other big cross-cutting change where the direction is rough and how it lands and how big it is aren't yet clear — to think it through, pressure-test it, and shape it toward a DECISION (which may be "no") plus a memorialized rationale and a phased plan, explicitly BEFORE any code. Signals: "let's think through", "talk it through before I commit", "is it worth it", "is this a good / dumb idea", "should we", "weigh the tradeoffs / what we'd gain and lose", "shape th… ([dbtlr/atlas-skills](https://github.com/dbtlr/atlas-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/shaping))
- **shaping**: Use when the problem is real and worth investing in — needs a solution shape, appetite, breadboard, or pitch. Triggers: 'shape this', 'write a pitch', 'scope this feature', 'set the appetite', 'breadboard this', 'parts and mechanisms', 'R x F fit', 'rabbit holes', 'turn this into a pitch'. If the problem feels poorly defined or symptomatic, route to diagnose first. Ends with a mandatory handoff prompt that writes the pitch to docs/shapeup-handoff/. ([dogndata/shapeup-claude-desktop](https://github.com/dogndata/shapeup-claude-desktop/tree/HEAD/skills/shaping))
- **shaping**: Shape ambiguous work by exploring requirements, solution mechanisms, fit checks and spikes before implementation. Use when the problem is clear enough to explore but the solution is not, such as a new page, interaction or layout idea. ([hellohynes/joshuahynes.com](https://github.com/hellohynes/joshuahynes.com/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/shaping) · 2 copies)
- **shaping**: Use when anything about to be planned or built is not 100% defined, at ANY stage of a project - a requirement, feature, or idea with decisions still open or that admits more than one interpretation; when you are about to fill a gap with your own assumption while implementing (catching yourself writing "I'll assume..." or picking something the user never decided IS the trigger); or before planning or coding anything not fully defined yet. The user may signal it with phrases like "it would be nice if...", "I was thinking maybe...", "something like...", but no special phrase is required. ([kitoep/sensei](https://github.com/kitoep/sensei/tree/HEAD/senior-engineer-en/skills/shaping))
- **shaping**: Use when an idea or change needs clearer outcomes, alternatives, constraints, terminology, or acceptance before implementation. ([mascah/skills](https://github.com/mascah/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/shaping))
- **shaping**: Shape requirements into a concise, plan-ready brief through collaborative dialogue before planning. ([simonwjackson/pi](https://github.com/simonwjackson/pi/tree/HEAD/packages/pi-thinking/skills/shaping))
- **shaping**: Collaboratively define a problem, compare materially different solution shapes, and select a solution before implementation planning. Use when requirements or approach are genuinely unsettled. ([SomtoUgeh/dotfiles](https://github.com/SomtoUgeh/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/shaping))
- **shaping**: 用于 rem Repo-wide 整形、维护与调优：resolver/authority/consumer drift、资产归位、经验保留与能力/成本评估。默认先 Inspect、report-first；执行须在 Player 授权 scope 内。具体 Motion 交 motioner，startup/daily handoff 交 rem-ready，叙事交 np0，确定性 MPS 交 teamspage。不用于普通单文件 coding、模型调度或替代 Human/EVAL acceptance。 ([terateams/rem](https://github.com/terateams/rem/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/shaping))
- **shaping-and-betting-work**: Turn a raw idea into a bettable piece of work bounded by a fixed *appetite* — the amount of time the idea is worth — rather than an open-ended estimate, and write it up as a pitch a team can bet on with confidence. - **shaping-and-shipping-software**: Guides software teams from product direction through scoped projects, clear issues, user feedback, and frequent launches. Use when setting goals, prioritizing work, shaping projects, writing issues, or planning execution and delivery. - **shaping-bets**: Shapes a milestone of a product context repository into bets: epic-sized, appetite-bounded work units with an observable outcome, the requirement IDs they cover, acceptance criteria, no-gos, rabbit holes, cuttable scope, the contracts and decisions they rely on, and an ordered context pack. - **shaping-breadboard**: Build affordance and wiring maps for existing or proposed systems before carding.
